1. VIC @0x38e00000: mask/default-vector init (+0x14=ff.., +0xf00/0x1f00,
   +0x2008/0x200c).
2. 0x3c800000 = 0xa5 (block unlock).
3. PMGR @0x3D100004 read, bit 0x40000 = warm-boot flag (clear = cold boot;
   SET would take the warm-resume branch at 0x20000164).
4. CLKCON @0x3C500000: full clock/PLL programming burst (+0x44/+0x48/+0x4c/
   +0x58/+0x68/+0x6c/+0x00/+0x20/+0x30/+0x08..+0x1c/+0x04).
5. Watchdog/timer block @0x39700000: +0x70=0x2582 (kick?), +0x6c, +0x18,
   +0x10=0x3e.
6. tmr @0x3C700000: counters at +0x84 AND +0xb4 (timeout loops use both);
   PLL at +0x88/+0x8c/+0x90; +0xa0/+0xa4/+0xa8/+0xb0 = timer channel ctrl.
7. FMC @0x38A00000 init/reset: +0x00=cs-config, +0x08=0xff (RESET cmd?),
   +0x48 = status (bit1 = cmd-done, write-1-to-clear), +0x04 = command
   (0x2a, 0xe0 seen), waits via 0x20000dd4(mask,timeout) helper.
8. Block @0x3C400000: pulse-init (+0x00/+0x08 strobes, +0x1c=4|6, +0x04=1,
   +0x34=4), status at +0x28 (bit5 must be set; then +0x40/+0x44/+0x100).
9. Block @0x38400000 = the FIRST boot medium driver polls: +0x10 =
   start/busy (write 1 -> wait bit0 clear + bit31 set), +0x14 = status/irq
   flags (bits 2,16,28,29,30 drive the detection state machine), command
   setup +0x08=0x2b, +0x0c=0x1708, +0x18=0x70000006, +0x24=0x218 (536 =
   length?), +0x28=0x01000218, +0x104=0x01000318. Possibly the real NAND
   controller on S5L8747 (FMC@0x38a00000 is the N3G-era block).

### Bootrom architecture (mapped)

- State struct ptr @ 0x2202fff8 (= 0x2202ba3c; matches wInd3x
  statePtrLocation). Key fields: +0x28 = media-detect busy flag,
  +0x29 = boot-ready flag (2 = boot!), +0x47 = USB/DFU active flag,
  +0xa0 = detection state, +0x24 = driver vtable (ROM @0x20000020).
- Main loop @0x20003d30: poll driver, check +0x29==2 -> jump to boot image
  via [[struct+0x3c]+0x20]; if !busy && !usb -> run detection step
  @0x20009f98 (pulses 0x3c400000) and retry.
- Detection state machine @0x2000a8fc: state 0 -> return immediately;
  states 5..11 handled @0x2000a944 (incl. [[vtable]+0x20] driver poll at
  0x2000a98c, retry/reset transitions on 0x38400014 bits).

### Stall

Detection never starts: busy flag (+0x28) is set before polling
0x3c400028 (bit5 — satisfied by ff-fill) but NEVER cleared afterwards;
the only clear site (0x2000ab34, in vtable fn 0x2000aad0) requires the

codex
- **Commands at `0x20003e24`** use USB SETUP byte **[1]**: `00 DETACH`, `01 DNLOAD`, `02 UPLOAD`, `03 GETSTATUS`, `04 CLRSTATUS`, `05 GETSTATE`, `06 ABORT`; others stall.
- SETUP `[2:4]` is the block number; `[6:8]` is little-endian length. DNLOAD accepts at most `0x800` bytes per request.
- **DNLOAD carries the image; GETSTATUS completes manifestation.** GETSTATUS requires length 6; GETSTATE requires length 1.
- `0x20003f24: strb 1,[transfer+0x10]` marks zero-length DNLOAD as final; `0x200042f0: strb 1,[state+0x29]` follows manifestation GETSTATUS.
- This is **not header acceptance**: image authentication occurs afterward through `0x20003bc8 → 0x200007d4`, before execution.

- **Image header**, relative to downloaded image start; payload starts at **`+0x400`**:
- `+0x00`: ASCII **`"8740"`**, bytes `38 37 34 30`; checked at `0x2000079c–0x200007ac`.
- `+0x07`: image type **3 or 4**, checked at `0x20000814–0x20000820`; type 3 additionally decrypts payload.
- Little-endian words: `+0x08` entry offset; `+0x0c` payload length; `+0x10` body length; `+0x14` certificate offset; `+0x18` certificate length.
- Let `P = align_up(payload_length,16)`: entry offset `≤P`; body length `≥P+0x300`; certificate offset `≥P+0x80` and divisible by 16 (`0x20000824–0x2000085c`).
- `+0x40`: 16-byte authentication value; `0x200006ac` hashes header bytes `[0,0x40)`, AES-processes the digest, and compares its first 16 bytes.
- Body signature occupies **payload+P**, length **`0x80`**; certificates start at **payload+certificate_offset**.
- Verification hashes the `0x400`-byte header and P payload bytes, then validates certificates and the RSA signature (`0x20000870–0x200008e8`).
- DNLOAD also runs reflected CRC-32, polynomial `0xedb88320`, initial `0xffffffff`; final four downloaded bytes must equal the raw running CRC (`0x200040e0–0x20004194`).

- **There is no 536-byte transfer here.** `0x2000a8d4: str r2,[r1,#0x24]` programs **GRXFSIZ=`0x218` FIFO words**; adjacent writes configure transmit FIFOs.
- These are **DWC2-style registers**, despite the supplied DWC3 identification; `0x218` carries no image or signature and receives no transfer validation.

- **GINTSTS sequence:** no `0→2→…→boot` path exists through the polling state machine.
- `0x2000aa30–0x2000aa48`: **bit30 (`0x40000000`)**, with bit0=1, selects state **2**; polling state 2 returns immediately.
- The same bit30 with **bit0=0 selects state 10**, enabling device handling. Initialization also selects state **8** when bit0=0.
- Maintain cable bit0 and PHY-ready bit5; GRSTCTL reset bit0 must clear and bit31 must become set (`0x2000a864–0x2000a898`).
- Device flow: **bit12 `0x1000` USB reset → bit13 `0x2000` enumeration done → EP0 enumeration requests**, including SET_ADDRESS and SET_CONFIGURATION(1).
- Each SETUP: **bit19 `0x80000`**, DAINT bit16=1, DOEPINT0 bit3=1; DMA the eight SETUP bytes to the programmed receive address (`0x2000a2f0–0x2000a330`).
- DNLOAD OUT completion: same bit19/DAINT, DOEPINT0 bit3=0; repeat in ≤64-byte packets (`0x2000a338–0x2000a3d8`).
- IN completion: **bit18 `0x40000`**, DAINT bit0=1; service response packets and status stages (`0x2000a2a4–0x2000a2ec`).
- After final GETSTATUS, OUT status completion with EP0 state≤1 changes boot-ready **1→2** (`0x2000a370–0x2000a378`); main loop then authenticates.
- **DFU is selected by descriptors/control traffic, not GINTSTS alone.** `0xfe` is the interface class matched during registration, not a dispatcher command.
- `0x20004488: strb r4,[r1,#0x47]`, with **r4=0**, clears that flag. Main-loop DFU-exit checking instead reads **`[state+0x630]+0x47`**.

- **Crypto MMIO:** AES **`0x38c00000`** (`0x200022f8`); SHA-1 **`0x38000000`** (`0x200030ec`); RSA modular-arithmetic engine **`0x3d000000`** (`0x2000865c`, called through `0x20008f28`).
- RSA verification checks **PKCS#1 v1.5/SHA-1 padding and digest**, plus certificate-chain trust (`0x20005b90`, `0x20005e38`). A correct magic and CRC alone cannot produce an authenticated boot.
tokens used
120,359
- **Commands at `0x20003e24`** use USB SETUP byte **[1]**: `00 DETACH`, `01 DNLOAD`, `02 UPLOAD`, `03 GETSTATUS`, `04 CLRSTATUS`, `05 GETSTATE`, `06 ABORT`; others stall.
- SETUP `[2:4]` is the block number; `[6:8]` is little-endian length. DNLOAD accepts at most `0x800` bytes per request.
- **DNLOAD carries the image; GETSTATUS completes manifestation.** GETSTATUS requires length 6; GETSTATE requires length 1.
- `0x20003f24: strb 1,[transfer+0x10]` marks zero-length DNLOAD as final; `0x200042f0: strb 1,[state+0x29]` follows manifestation GETSTATUS.
- This is **not header acceptance**: image authentication occurs afterward through `0x20003bc8 → 0x200007d4`, before execution.

- **Image header**, relative to downloaded image start; payload starts at **`+0x400`**:
- `+0x00`: ASCII **`"8740"`**, bytes `38 37 34 30`; checked at `0x2000079c–0x200007ac`.
- `+0x07`: image type **3 or 4**, checked at `0x20000814–0x20000820`; type 3 additionally decrypts payload.
- Little-endian words: `+0x08` entry offset; `+0x0c` payload length; `+0x10` body length; `+0x14` certificate offset; `+0x18` certificate length.
- Let `P = align_up(payload_length,16)`: entry offset `≤P`; body length `≥P+0x300`; certificate offset `≥P+0x80` and divisible by 16 (`0x20000824–0x2000085c`).
- `+0x40`: 16-byte authentication value; `0x200006ac` hashes header bytes `[0,0x40)`, AES-processes the digest, and compares its first 16 bytes.
